# Rows above main

One table for every row above main that a landing opens, so the tuning phase starts from a complete list. Compile-time losses are rows too (metric `Ir`; there `main` is the pin). Workers add their rows in their PRs; w-6 merges and de-duplicates. `tools/test_above_main_rows.py` refuses a row without an owner and an exit.

Metric: `clocks` (486 model, `tools/rows/`), `bytes` (object or LEDATA), `Ir` (callgrind or `perf instructions:u`). Delta is parity minus main. A date in `exit` is the day the row is re-measured or closed.

Fix: a nested-loop trip estimator from the outer range (the pin's BFI/SCEV path), branch `alim/6502-nested-trip-estimator`. Exit: the estimator lands or the cell is within x1.2 Tried 2026-11-23 (tip 5b003be8bb): (1) a finite O2/O3 inline rate on the static remark: `main -> marks` saves 24 clk for 88 B = 0.27 clk/B, N=1 accepts none; (2) pricing the finished trial by `weights::run_cost`: kept 48,039 clk / 464 B, inlined 268,791 clk / 650 B estimated (real net 29,272 and 16,429): a sign flip, because the kept side guesses 10 trips where the copy knows 98; (3) `inline_hints_known_loop_bound` for 6502: 0 of 648 cells change, `marks` is excluded by the guards at inlinecost.rs:1433 (bound over the full-unroll threshold, not the innermost loop; widening re-opens idiv_loop_os +128 B, switch +402 B). Forcing the inline gives 523 B / 16,429 clk net (llvm-mos 646 B / 17,124). Cause (reconciled 2026-11-23): the rate veto: the pin-path inline of `marks` is proposed (`trial inline: 1 sites, 0 kept`) and the byte-shrink trial rejects it (295 -> 523 B, -12,843 clk = 56 clk saved a byte); an estimator only prices it once a rate exists. Oversight question: the clk/B rate for O1-O3 inline and unroll trials. | w9 | 2026-12-18 | |

Rate (clock + 3 B per clock-equivalent, min layout, 6502 O1): long_compare_chain +9,221, known_loads_walks +4,436, bit_test +9: worse at the rate, so rows | a select whose branch reads Z or N (`beq`, `bne`, `bmi`, `bpl`) keeps its head move before the compare (a load or a transfer sets N and Z), so with A live the move goes through a register: `ldy #0; cmp #0; beq; ldy #1; tya` where the one-instruction select was `bne; txa; rts`, and in a loop `pha;txa;tay;pla`; carry branches (`bcc`, `bcs`) are not affected (`spill::COMPARE`). The head copy before the compare is the pin's ([X86InstrInfo.cpp#L5640](https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/blob/d1106deb71cc81016406a1917d0508d2df296266/llvm/lib/Target/X86/X86InstrInfo.cpp#L5640): x86 hoists `MOV32r0` above the compare); the defect for bit_test is the return join `tya; rts`. Early tail duplication does not apply: the pin refuses a return before allocation ([TailDuplicator.cpp#L643](https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/blob/d1106deb71cc81016406a1917d0508d2df296266/llvm/lib/CodeGen/TailDuplicator.cpp#L643)) and the shared `EarlyTailDuplicate` on the 6502 LIR copies 0 blocks (probe 2026-11-10, 65C02: bit_test 4 -> 4 blocks, memcpy_libcall 22 -> 22, idioms `main` 137 -> 136 and 1 insn). General fix: post-RA duplication of a short return tail into its predecessors, as the pin's late TailDuplicate does (shared `latetaildup` on an allocated LirBody of the 6502 driver, step 5; hand count 17 B = the old size; not shown for long_compare_chain / known_loads_walks); card `6502-a-bit_test-cause-card-2026-11-10.md` | 6502-c | 2026-11-27 | #8729 |

| 6502-FAS-CLK | frame_address_stack_args (`tests/run/c`) | Os | clocks (code bytes better) | release/next a1d720aafc (6502-llc 569aa432) | perf/6502-trial-bytes 3aa1a3c467 (6502-llc 3216a08f) | 6502 -60 B (1168 -> 1108), 1950 -> 12201 clk (6.3x; 256 pads 1953 -> 12250); 65c02 -61 B, 1946 -> 12165 (256 pads 1949 -> 12214); sim65 PASS | the SZ2 trial of `kernel` into `line` read -99 B and now reads +99 B (calibrated `function_bytes`), so it is refused; `line` keeps its call and its inline cost at `main` goes from -20 to 10 (Os bar 10), so `line` stays a call. Inlined, the chain folded on the constant arguments: `main` was two `report(constant)` calls, now it runs `__mulsi3` three times per `line`. Os is strict bytes; the clock axis is worse. Ruling (oversight 11-11): Os bytes win, the clock loss is the record. A win on both axes would be a trial that keeps the constant folding without the size (a model that sees constant operands of a multiply); best of the others (ext.sh, floor-subtracted, no-print clocks, output checked, 2026-11-11): llvm-mos a40207a371b6 327 cy and 126 B with section GC, which drops the four unreferenced external functions; 1187 B with `--no-gc-sections`, like ours and cc65 (864 B / 16,823 cy); SDCC UNMEASURED (no 6502 harness). Ours Os 1108 B / 12,200 clk (before 1168 B / 1950 clk): 79 B below llvm-mos like for like, 37x its clocks. llvm-mos inlines the chain into main and folds the report arguments, which costs bytes while the externals stay; the byte axis is not behind | 6502-c | review on a bytes-and-clocks win (oversight 11-11: Os stays strict on bytes) | #9138 |

| AM-CZ-1 | x_bubble m32 | O2,O3 | clocks | 74487 | 88533 | +14046 (+18.9%); Os 81513 to 95665 (+17.4%) | the inner loop: main's gcc-style LSR counts it to zero over a biased pointer (6 instructions a trip); the LLVM LSR, pin-equal at the IR, leaves `inc; cmp; jne` with an IV copy (8 a trip; the pin's llc 7). The zero-end formula needs a start of minus the variable bound, with the address sharing that counter: a formula neither the pin nor ours generates (its ICmpZero formulas are `bound - i` and a scale -1); #3547 adds +4.3% to it; 2026-11-10 I1 = m32-owner-table-bytes-2026-11-10-codedata.tsv (x86-m32-bytes, release/next 9c8ce47716): O2/O3 CLOSED (66688 vs main 74487, below main). Os open: 95810 vs main 81513 (+17.5%; gcc 88537, clang 94491). Os cause (greedy-os-causes, harness --hist, dist at release/next a034ebb44e): not the count-to-zero formula (ours is `inc; cmp reg,reg; jne` as gcc's). The inner trip carries a[j+1] into the next trip (GVN load PRE phi): the swap arm (51% of 7,260 trips) ends in a taken `jmp` over the carry copy `mov ebx,ebp` (JMP 3,703 / 11,109 clk vs gcc 359 / 1,077) = 11.1k clk, 11.6% of the cell. Tail-duplicating the 3-instruction latch into the swap arm removes it (+5 B); gcc's copy_bb_p refuses at -Os (grows the code) and the pin's tail-dup size is 1 at optsize, so the byte axis wins and the cell stays. `-fno-tree-pre` is worse (97,006 clk, 171 B) | x86-m32 | 2026-11-20 | #3547, #8905 |

The pin's ranges `lo` [0, 64), `hi` [0, 65) make `mid` a `lshr` and InstCombine's `canEvaluateZExtd` widens `values[i] & mid` to i32 (`zext nneg`), as the pin does at m32: `mid` is live twice across the call, 16-bit for `chop(mid, hi)` and 32-bit for the loop (a dword frame slot, `mov dword ptr [bp-8], esi`). Pin llc -O1/-O2 on our final IR emits the same imul, so it is the pin's LSR on this loop (owner w12 / m32-o1-small POC: -3200 clk at +5 B, bar slack 6). (b) 400: the atn load common to both arms is hoisted before the branch (SimplifyCFG late slot, `hoistCommonCodeFromSuccessors`; the pin does the same), so neither arm folds it, the inner loop needs a 7th register and the outer loop spills sum and i (4 stack moves an iteration against 2). Probe with one load per arm: -397 clk, -8 B code, spills 2 -> 1. MachineCSE heuristics 1 and 3 (the pin's) stop sharing the page-offset `add` across blocks: it is made again in each (9 `#lo12` adds, main 1). Pin llc on the same IR is 348 B / 77 i at O3 and keeps one `MOVaddr` for all blocks (llc -print-after=machine-cse): not pin-faithful. Cause: heuristic 3 reads a copy into a multi-def register as a PHI use; in the pin a constant or address a phi takes goes through SelectionDAG's export `COPY`, so the PHI is not its user. Not the allocator (2026-11-10): the pin's greedy given the merged vreg also takes x20 (pin llc -start-after=machine-cse on our IR, the two 30011 defs hand-merged: same `stp x20, x19` frame); the pin avoids it only by never merging them. A narrower carve-out, merging a cheap def at -Os unless a call lies on a path def -> use, closes recpow (192 -> 188) but makes jpeg_fixed +24, jpeg_single +8, stft_fixed +12, stft_single +24 (36-program -Os A/B), so a lever needs register-pressure knowledge (a CSR already in use), beyond the pin. | ARM64-OS-5 | switch / matmul arm64 (llrm-cc, -fomit-frame-pointer) | Os | kernel bytes | gcc 252 / 280 (clang 320 / 496; pin 320 / 304) | 256 / 288 | +4 (+1.6%) / +8 (+2.9%) against gcc | gaps to gcc, ours below the pin. lru left the row 2026-11-09: gcc built -fPIE (ours' default) is 356 B, ours 356 B, same. switch (owner w15, exit 2026-11-30): a gap to gcc, beyond the pin, not a missing pass. gcc ends the 7-case compare chain of `route` with `cmp x1, 0; csinc x1, x14, xzr, eq` (its own if-conversion of the last case into the default, 1 = xzr + 1); ours keeps `cmp; b.ne default; mov w, #N; b join`. The pin does not convert it either: pin llc on our post-mid-end IR with -stress-early-ifcvt, and with -mcpu=apple-m1 / cortex-a76, gives identical .text (0x168) and no csel/csinc/ccmp (`SSAIfConv::canConvertIf` needs a 2-predecessor tail and the join has many); bytes ours 256, gcc 252, pin llc 320. Lever (gcc side): a select-forming step for single-case leaves of the lowered compare tree; card by 2026-11-14 only if it is general, else the row stays recorded. matmul (owner the arm64 judge session, exit 2026-11-30): `bench_matmul` is 72 instrs ours against gcc's 70 (+8 B). Not an induction-variable widening (ours' IVs are already 64-bit; ldrsh x/w and madd/smaddl are the same size). The cost is +4 moves (copies of loop-invariant bases into per-loop pointer registers before the loops, `orr x13, xzr, x10`), offset by 2 fewer branches; the lever is machine-level coalescing, not found at IR level. Pin llc (-O2, pic, also apple-m1) on our post-mid-end IR (`LLRM_MIR_STAGES`, last stage) is 76 instrs = 304 B, 16 B above ours, so no pin pass does better on this IR; the pin's own clang flow is 304 B too. Cause of the 8 B to gcc, by region: init nest +2 instrs (ours recomputes `seed+1+3i` with two adds per outer iteration and keeps a third IV for `i`; gcc steps `w0 += 3`), the rest are the same counts; gcc's IVopts choice, not a missing translation. Lever: the `LsrModel` A/B (ARM64-OS-3, after w12's trait). Measured on release/next a5a8f0d252 dist, 2026-11-10: ours 288 B, gcc 280, clang 496. | ARM64-RECGCD-O2 | recgcd arm64 (llrm-cc, -fomit-frame-pointer) | O2 | kernel instrs / bytes | gcc 17,314 i / 132 B (clang 17,611 i / 120 B) | 17,611 i / 112 B | +297 i (+1.7%) against gcc, equal to clang | per-pc counts (stamped tip ba096315a2): both sides run the same blocks except the first `gcd(i*7+3, i*5+2)` loop. gcc enters it past the loop-top copy `mov w2,w1` (899 runs against 1,199 for the other four instrs) and puts its two start copies in the preheader; ours copies at the top every pass (5-instr loop in ours, gcc and pin llc). Net +297 instrs, not 300 free copies: gcc pays extra IV registers. Cause shown (stamped tip ba096315a2; local diagnostics in `why_left` and `JoinVals::map_values`, not committed, output jg-why.out and jg-why2.out): `LLRM_COALESCE_WHY=1` leaves 6 copies, all "conflict (deferred)"; the loop-top copy is block 4 `v19 <- v42` (id 23). Its join fails in `map_values` on a real overlap, not a coalescer quirk: v19 (the phi value `a`, also the loop's live-out) is live over [432r,816r); v42 (the phi temp of `b`) has the back-edge copy `v42 <- v43` defined at 512r inside that range (v42: [384r,416B) [416B,432r) [512r,576B)). The back-edge copy sits before the latch branch, so it executes on the exit path while v19 is still live: v19 and v42 interfere. The pin does not split that edge either: PHIElimination.cpp#L827-832 skips loop back edges ("small out-of-line blocks into the loop ... bad for code placement"; https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/blob/d1106deb71cc81016406a1917d0508d2df296266/llvm/lib/CodeGen/PHIElimination.cpp#L827), and pin llc on our final IR keeps the copy (17,611 executed, as pin clang). gcc keeps the copy on the back edge (`cbnz w1, 2c` to `2c: mov w2,w1`, 899 runs) and enters the loop past it. So no pin pass reaches gcc's; the lever would be splitting a back edge whose phi source is live-out, which reverses the pin's choice: a DEVIATE, to be priced against code placement. Not shown: whether w15's fib O2 "conflict (deferred)" (multi-def argument vreg) is the same cause; this overlap is a different mechanism, so do not assume it | llrm-arm64-judge-gaps | 2026-11-30 | |

| Z80-SLOT-READ-PEEK | crc16 Oz, bsearch8 O2 (tests `a_function_whose_values_fit_in_registers_makes_no_frame_access`, `a_search_loop_keeps_its_base_pointer_in_a_register`, both `#[ignore]`d with this id); frameless O2 (row Z80-FRAMELESS-O2) | Oz, O2 | bytes, clocks | crc16 77 B vs SDCC 59; bsearch8 68 B / 790 T vs SDCC 56 / 702 | tip | frame access remains (`cp a,-1(ix)` and a pointer in a push/pop slot) | crc16 -Os/-Oz: the pin's LSR skips a non-innermost loop (LoopStrengthReduce.cpp, translated as is), so `data[i]` is not reduced while the 8-trip loop stays, and 7 bytes are live in it (crc 2, counter, i, pointer 2, length) against 6 byte registers: one spill is forced (the length, `cp a,-1(ix)`); at -O2 the inner loop unrolls, LSR reduces the pointer and the code has 0 frame references. bsearch8: no LSR use (binary search). In both a pair parked in a LIFO push/pop slot is read by peek (`pop hl; push hl`, 21 T and 2 B each trip) where the pin would keep it in a register or a free copy: the spill weights do not price the slot form (no pin counterpart for push/pop slots; SDCC prices each instruction, `z80/ralloc2.cc`). Tried: `spill_weight_scale` 2.0 for pairs (the hook's stated meaning): 212 of 555 outputs differ, 24 cells better, 27 worse, the two tests unchanged: rejected. Next: a per-reference reload cost (`reference_weight`, x86 m16 precedent) — card slot-read-peek-card-2026-11-10.md | llrm-target-z80 | 2026-11-30 | next PR |

| WSZ-32 | shrink_wrapped_saves wasm32 (`tests/run/c`) | O1 | bytes (instrs 353 vs 361) | 246 | 257 | +11 | LSR use order, not the inliner (`f` is inlined into `g` in both, and our stage-28 IR equals the pin's): the pin's `llc -O1` on its own bitcode gives 246 B, on text IR (parse order) 257 B, ours 257 B. The pin's use-list order of the loop counter (`uselistorder i32 %5, {5,3,2,1,0,4,6}`) is pipeline history; ours differs, so the compare use comes second (the pin's sixth) and the cross-use formula set has `(147+%0)` where the pin has `-147 + (3*%0) + {147,+,3}`; the search then picks 17 instrs against the pin's 14. Same cause as IVU-ORDER-1 (pass not identified there either). No fixing branch or PR exists for use lists in LLVM order (IU1 exit); use lists in LLVM order (IU1) assigned to llrm-w9-debug-lines 2026-11-16 | llrm-w9-debug-lines | 2026-11-30 | this PR |

| M64-BIND-1 | tail_calls (m64 -fPIE / -fno-pic / unflagged); the 40 m64 cells #8696 shortened | O1,O2,O3,Os,Oz | R_X86_64_PLT32 on a call or jump to a same-object global (and 3 B per shortened jump) | pin 25 (clang -fPIE 25) | 9 | -16 relocations, below the pin (#8696); gcc -fPIE (-fno-inline) 26 / 19 / 19 / 19 at O1 / O2 / O3 / Os | DEVIATE 1 in objbuild.rs. The pin's MC resolves a PC-relative reference only to STB_LOCAL (ELFObjectWriter.cpp L1391), so it leaves every global to the linker even when a PIE or static link cannot interpose it; ours binds a definition no other module can replace (`masm::Module::preemptible` lists the ones that can: weak, and default-visibility at -fPIC) and gcc/gas do the same for an executable. #8696 binds the resolved sibling tails (25 -> 9 at -fPIE). #8735 keeps PLT32 for what another module can replace: tail_calls -O2 -fPIC 9 -> 25, 44 programs 157 -> 169 PLT32, -fPIE 9 / 157 unchanged (same binaries, 8dace0f22 vs 2de7ad85af); bytes at -fPIE: 0 of 176 cells moved. At -fPIC ours is the pin's: 25 relocations, gcc -fno-inline 25, clang 25 (same flags). Checked end to end on x86_64 and x86_32 against the pin and gcc: weak and, at -fPIC, default-visibility globals keep PLT32; hidden and static bind | x86-m64 | 2026-12-10, review; leaves when the bar is no longer gcc or the pin binds locally | #8696, #8735 |

Cause: x86-64 `timings.times` is PROVISIONAL Skylake-class figures (its header says so); a back edge is priced ~3.8 clk a trip. Pricing the nest changes no byte (tried). The table is not the cause: `machine/tune/x86/x86-64.toml` already translates the pin's SandyBridgeModel (the pin's model for generic x86-64); SkylakeClient differs in idiv r32 (40 to 26) and cmov latency (2 to 1) only, and swapping them changed jpeg_single Os/O2/O3 and nbody O3 (fewer bytes and instructions) and no cell's verdict (2026-11-13, 4-level counted sweep). Fix: a calibration of the back-edge term, if the A/B shows the overshoot is systemic, is a DEVIATE UP1 with the same exit | x86-m64 | 2026-12-20, back-edge term calibrated | #8829 |

| M32-MUL-O1B | x_mul_constants x86-m32 | O1 | bytes and clocks | bars 1927 B (clang) / 142,036 clk (gcc) | 1708 B / 286,733 clk (imul), or 2219 B / 132,333 clk (chains) | -219 B / +144,697 clk, or +292 B / -9,703 clk | no point at or below both bars. The shipped imuls are 32 x `x * 1664525` (one per function) and three small ones (125, 1000, 100); the pin takes the rest. Real chains: 1664525 9 clk / 23 B for the fewest-clock and the fewest-byte ranking alike (imul 6 B / 31 clk), 125 / 1000 / 100 at 9-12 B. Lower bound on any chain (mulchains.py: none of <= 5 ops; with >= 6 ops and one 3-byte op the growth cannot reach 2^20.7): >= 14 B / >= 6 clk, +8 B and <= 25 clk saved per trip. The clock bar needs 144,697 clk: <= 5,000 per function from the 1664525 site, so >= 28 functions even with the three small sites chained (+18 B): >= +242 B against +219 B of slack. Dominance rule (a chain only where no longer and faster): changed no object over 6,000 `int` and `short` constants at -O1 and -Os; the INL-O1 rate (156 mclk/B) reproduces the fewest-clock chains (x_fir 188 -> 221 B, above both bars) | x86-m32-bytes | 2026-11-17 | |

| M32-O1-ADLER | x_adler x86-m32 | O1 | clocks | gcc 47,974 clk (160 B; clang 57,094 clk, 115 B) | 57,094 clk, 111 B | +9,120 clk (+19%) | equal to clang to the clock (the pin's code). `--hist`: ours 13,526 `mov` against gcc's 3,005: asm diffed 2026-11-13: ours `mov edx,eax; sub edx,0xfff1; jae L; mov edx,eax; L:` (4 insns when no subtract is needed, the common case), gcc `cmp edx,0xfff0; jbe L; sub edx,0xfff1; L:` (2). The IR input has a branch around the `sub`; SimplifyCFG `speculativelyExecuteBB`/`foldTwoEntryPHINode` makes it `select(a > 65520, a - 65521, a)` and the i486 (no CMOV) lowers the select to a branch diamond with the `sub` before it (EmitLoweredSelect); the pin's clang -march=i486 emits the same code. A/Bs on 121 programs x O1/Os/O2/O3, all refused: (a) no one-armed speculation without CMOV: x_adler unchanged, counted 63/85/37/37 -> 63/85/37/38; (b) no two-entry fold where a select is a branch: Os 85 -> 86 (+x_adler, x_primes; -sfm16_float), O1 63 -> 68 (-x_crc32 +22,535 clk +19 B), O2 37 -> 38 and O3 37 -> 39 (-x_crc32); (c) fold only where the select lowers branchless (two constants, or `x op K` against `x`): O1 65 -> 66 (+x_collatz), Os 85 -> 85, O2/O3 37 -> 36 (-x_crc32 +13,963 clk +116 B) and x_adler unchanged. x_crc32's select (a 50/50 xor) wants the fold and x_adler's (rare) wants the branch; with no profile the two differ only by branch probability, which is SelectOptimize's `isSelectHighlyPredictable` (ported, unwired: the pin's llc disables it, TargetPassConfig.cpp:278), not by shape. | llrm-target-x86-m32-b | 2026-11-24 | - |

The other cells were not diffed Probes 2026-11-13 (tip 168cf6aed1, vsgcc harness, output ok, discarded): FF2's `fld1`/`fldz` fold forced on at m32 O1 past both byte gates moves 5 of 13 cells: x_ld_dot, fma_chain, sum, max -32 clk, +6 B (code +2, new pool cell +4); matmul -576 clk, +6 B; against the clock bars sum stays +17, dot +22, fma_chain +22, matmul +95: no flip alone, and the layout part (b, +21..+37 clk, outer-loop `jmp`+`je`) is parked (the pin's guard probability needs a tuned constant). The shadow IV (OptimizeShadowIV + LS-SH) off on x87 moves axpy, horner, poly, scale, to_double, to_float by 14-54 clk at +2..+9 B: axpy flips its clock bar (26845 vs 26857) and fails its byte bar (230 vs 226). dot, fma_chain, sum, newton, matmul, max, cmp3 do not move. Hot loops equal gcc's in dot, horner, scale, sum, poly; the gap is the 8-iteration init section. Also, from the stale #9686 (x86-m32-b, 2026-11-13): `--hist` 2026-11-13 tip bacfb674df against gcc (x_ld_to_double, sum, scale, dot): loop control is not the gap (DEC 512 + JNE vs CMP 520 + JNE: equal clocks); the hot loops match. Two causes in the shared bench skeleton `for r<8 { t += k(); x[r] += 1; }`: (a) `x[r] += 1` is ours `fld1; faddp` (12 clk) against gcc `fadd dword [const]` (8 clk, +2 B): +32 clk (sum, dot, scale); (b) outer-loop layout: the latch comes first, so a `jmp` and a `je` run every outer iteration (+21..+37 clk; to_double/to_float/poly +21 are all this); gcc keeps the guard branch cold and falls through. Both are the pin's own output: pin llc -O1 on our final IR prints the same layout and `fld1; faddp` (`MachineBlockPlacement::rotateLoop` L2423 puts the exit block at the bottom). Fix is beyond the pin: (b) traced with `-debug-only=block-placement`: `findBestLoopTop` moves the latch above the header because `FallThroughGains` is 28 against 25, the guard `n>0` being estimated 62.5% taken (ZeroHeuristic) when it is always taken; the layout flips only above ~84% (pin llc with `!prof` weights 73/27 and 90/10 keep it, 99/1 gives gcc's), and gcc's `PRED_LOOP_GUARD` is 73%, so no faithful rule flips it and a tuned constant would be row-shaped; (a) an x87 rule that takes a constant as a memory operand where it executes less (not at -Os). By delta, (b) alone flips 5 cells (+3, +8, +21 x3), (a)+(b) up to 9; not built. x87 keeper 2026-11-13 (clean release/next 78e54ef9da, 35 x87 programs): (b) is the x87 edge block, not the guard odds: MachineSink sinks `fldz` into a split critical edge (the pin never sinks x87 pseudos: `Uses = [FPCW]`). Modelling the FPCW read gives the pin's layout, but the pin then runs `fstp st(0); fldz` every outer iteration: x_ld_sum/dot/fma_chain/horner +35..41 clk at O1/O2/Os, O3 unchanged, 0 cells flip; dropped. O2 dot/fma_chain/sum are +20..24 rate (this `jmp`); O3 dot/fma_chain/sum are +80..90 B, no layout cause; O2 horner is +137 rate: instcombine sinks the 8 `t += r` adds into the exit, 8 live sums > 7 x87 slots, one spill (O3 unrolls in another order); stft_single O1 (+344k clk) is the same byte trade as (a) | x86-m32-b | 2026-11-24 | - |

NaN : x` select, branch-lowered at i486: no cmov) on every one of 400 iterations (MOV 796 vs gcc 405 = +391 clk, the whole +385); gcc puts the constant on the NaN arm only (`mov eax,esi` after `jne`) and the in-loop `sahf` of ours is 2 clk against its `and ah,0x45; xor ah,0x40` 2 clk. Ours = the pin clang instruction for instruction (14,809): the constant is a PHI operand, and the pin's MachineLICM does not hoist a cheap instruction with a loop PHI use (MachineLICM.cpp L1292-1298) nor sink it onto the edge: beyond pin, no pass to translate; a lever would split the select's critical edge and place the copy on the NaN edge (general: partially dead PHI copy), 2 cells (add, mul) at O1  2026-11-23 (tip d653b1a462, vsgcc harness): every O1 cell of this row is now under its bar: sfm16_float_add 13,619 clk (best 14,427), mul 16,412 (17,227), sub 14,012 (15,202), div 40,410 (41,600), x_nbody_f 373,259 (388,472); the NaN-arm constant no longer runs in the loop (the LICM work, #10677/#10680). The Os cells of the same programs are separate (table 2026-11-23: sfm16_float_add/sub/mul Os +2.7/+2.6/+2.2%). Os cells of the same programs stay open (vsgcc 2026-11-23, tip d653b1a462; best = gcc): sfm16_float_add 14,809 vs 14,423 clk (+386), sub +386, mul +386, x_nbody_f +0.6%: `--hist` MOV 796 against the O1 build's 403: at Os the NaN arm's `mov eax,0x7fc00000` sits before the `jp` and runs every trip (no tail duplication at Os, so the join keeps one tail; O2 and O1 place it on the NaN arm only); the pin's clang -Os is the same stream as ours (14,809), gcc places the PHI copy on the edge. Lever (beyond the pin): place a PHI copy of a constant on the unlikely edge (critical-edge split at PHI elimination, gcc out-of-SSA edge insertion), general for any select-of-constant with a rare arm; not built, needs a bytes check (the split edge block adds a `jmp`). | x87-cordic-2 | 2026-12-06 | - |

Loop 1 (1,024 stores): clang counts `ecx` from -4096 to 0 and stores at `[esp+ecx+0x1000]`, ending on the `add`'s flags; ours keeps a pointer in `ecx` and `eax` as the counter, ending `cmp eax,0xbfc` (+1 `cmp` per iteration). Cause: `CallFrameOpt` prices the push against the frame's kept alignment (`Registers::align`, a slot, 4 here: no quad is held), the pin against the ABI's 16 (`TFL->getStackAlign()`: the realigning `sub` makes a one-word push unprofitable). | 6502-PTRFOLD-2 | the 65C02 `(p)` form is chosen at print time (65C02) | O2,Os | bytes, clocks | n/a (deviation) | n/a | Y-free proof of concept, 2026-11-10 (patch `6502-a-ptrfold2-poc.patch`, not pushed): differential over 139 programs, 29 bench kernels, 28 hand kernels x 6502/65c02 x O2/Os: NMOS 0 files differ, 65C02 only memory_expansion changes; Os -27 B, -116890 clk (executed, 16 pads); O2 does not link (6502-LINK-MEMEXP), +51 insns read from the non-linking asm. No other cell frees Y | offset 0 on a 65C02 prints `op (p)`; the allocator still models Y as written, so `ldy #0` is dropped but Y is not freed. Freeing it moves one cell both ways, so it stays a deviation. The pin picks the addressing mode in isel ([X86ISelDAGToDAG.cpp#L692](https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/blob/d1106deb71cc81016406a1917d0508d2df296266/llvm/lib/Target/X86/X86ISelDAGToDAG.cpp#L692)) | 6502-a | 2026-12-01 | #8748 |

## Recorded, not worse than the bar

No owner or exit: a cell under the best external at its judging metric is not a worse cell. It reopens if the bar moves under ours.

- **RECPOW-M64-OS** (2026-11-13): recpow x86_64-linux Os, main 65098fd950 `.text` 217 B -> 189 B (-28 B). A first A/B read +4 B because its `size` column counted `.eh_frame` (120 -> 152 B: main inlined `power` into one function, the tip keeps it with its own FDE); the bar is code bytes. The count-down loop is the rotation trial (kept: -3 B). Instrument fixed: the A/B takes `.text` only.

## D-row: expression-carrying debug records and `salvageDebugInfo` (AM-DBG-1)

Design only; no code before the coordinator's go.

**What the pin keeps.** `observed.c` at -O2 -g through clang-20 leaves `#dbg_value` records among the code, each with a `DIExpression`: `plus_uconst`, `constu N; mul|shl; stack_value`, `LLVM_fragment` for the pieces of `v`, `p`, `q`, and `DIArgList` for a value of several operands. When a pass deletes or folds an instruction, `salvageDebugInfo` (Utils/Local.cpp, `salvageDebugInfoImpl`) rewrites the records that used it as an expression over its operands (an add of a constant becomes `plus_uconst`, a shl `constu; shl`, a cast `convert`) instead of killing them. Result on this fixture: 18 values read like -O0, none wrong.

**Ours.** `DebugWhat` (llrm-mir `module.rs`) is `Value(operand)`, `Piece`, `GonePiece`, `Gone`, `Declare`: a bare value, no expression. `Function::erase` of a value's last definition ends in `Gone`. So `c = n * 6` folded into `mul %0, 6` is lost with its variable. The output side already writes constants and pieces (`NoteValue`, `DW_OP_piece`).

**Minimal slice that revives a, b, c, i** (three PRs):
1. MIR: `DebugWhat::Value` gains `expr: Vec<ExprOp>` (`PlusUconst`, `Constu`, `Mul`, `Shl`, `Plus`, `StackValue`); printer, parser and verifier read it. Pieces unchanged.
2. Salvage: a `Function::salvage(inst)` for single-operand cases (add/sub/mul/shl by constant, zext/sext/trunc), called where InstCombine, IndVars, LoopDeletion and SimplifyCFG erase or replace a value (the pin's call sites); everything else stays `Gone`. No `DIArgList` in this slice.
3. Backend: `NoteValue::Expr(vreg, ops)` in isel `noted`, and the DWARF writer emits `DW_OP_bregN 0; ops; DW_OP_stack_value` (CodeView keeps the plain value).

**Cost** (estimate, not measured): about 700 lines and 3 PRs; compile time unchanged without `-g` (records exist only then); objects identical with and without `-g` (the `g_leaves_*` tests guard it). Done when `a_variable_reads_the_same_at_o2_as_at_o0...` passes at floor 18 and loses its `ignore`.

## m68k onto `machine_with` (owner m68k-lsr-addressing, ruling 2026-11-09; closes JT-O1-1..3)

Estimates, not measurements; CSE comes through the shared flow. Gap card: `m68k-onto-machine-with-gaps-2026-11-09.md`. Every step: x86 md5 identity, m68k A/B bytes and clocks (68000/68020, O1/O2/Os), compile time linear at 600 and 1200 functions.

- 2026-11-09 (done): pre-phi `MFunction` to `LirBody` and back (null stage equal on 18 functions x 2 CPUs); the shared `precse` and `MachineLICM` run on it, both off. `machine_with` builds for m68k.
- 2026-11-09 attribution: hosted CSE and the pin's direct `CopyFromReg` are one effect (68000 O2 +394 B, 16 of 20 worse cells shared): the pin's MIR shape on private passes tuned on copies (shiftadd, addrroles, loopinc, allocator hints). CSE stays off until they are shape-robust or retired by step 4.
- 2026-11-10 (done): the converter makes each physical write its own pinned value (`4f9cee96bf`, m68k-machine-with): 20 of 20 fixture functions pass `flow::checked`; `from_ssa` for new copies and blocks is next.
- JT-O1-1..3 against the external bar (sieve -O1, whole-object .text, gcc 15.2.0 / clang 20.1.8, 2026-11-10): bytes ok (ours 164/154/154 against gcc 166/162/162); clocks worse (68000 289112 against 189350, +53%; 68020 +10.8%; 68040 not priced), cause `i*i` before the `i<32` guard, owner llrm-target-m68k. The rows stay open until the main-column difference is fixed.
- 2026-11-12 20:00Z: report (this list, per-pass losses, the verdicts).
- 2026-11-18: step 4a: shared PhiElimination and TwoAddress hosted (`from_ssa` takes new copies, renamed defs, new vregs); private `eliminate_phis_with`, `fold_reserved_copies`, `commute_tied`, `two_address`, `machine_sink` retired; m68k classes, rules and the omitted-phase list from the target description.
- 2026-11-24: step 4b: the copy-shape cells re-measured (CSE and `direct_copies` on); the private passes that still lose a cell are made shape-robust or replaced. Part of the cost is decided before phi elimination: `addrroles`/`addrarith` put a value in a data register once its copy is gone (nbody), so step 4a does not retire it; the class choice moves to the allocator, as in the pin. The omitted-phase list stays open (x86-only phases inert is unproven: no frame, calls, rules or verifier in the test).
- 2026-11-30: post-RA copyprop, branch folding, block placement shared.
- 2026-12-04: frame/epilogue from `FrameRegisters`; replaced private passes deleted; JT-O1-1..3 closed.

Files, one writer each (merge direction one way: m68k-machine-with branches off `alim/m68k-machine-with` b654939e04):
- m68k-lsr-addressing: this section, the first-running-step report (2026-11-12 20:00Z), the A/B scripts (`m68k-mw`); no code from b654939e04 on.
- m68k-machine-with: step 3 and on: `llrm-spike-mf/src/tolir.rs`, `llrm-codegen/src/backend/precse.rs`, `llrm-m68k/src/{mfunc,regalloc,compile}.rs`, their tests.

Where: +1200 PUSH and +1200 POP (1.5 per call) and +1697 MOV. Read from the objects on the same IR: the pin saves 3 registers in __gedf2 and 3 (+1 slot) in __cmpdf2, one exit each, no shrink-wrapping; ours saves 4 and 3, one exit; gcc15 pushes 2 and returns through several exits. So the lever is gcc's early-exit placement (beyond the pin); the pin-comparable residue is one extra saved register in __gedf2 (objects only, the ShrinkWrap/CSR debug not read: UNVERIFIED as a decision). Of the 8 ins: ~3-4 the 0.79 early-return path (gcc copies the 4-insn latch into it, no reloads, no `jmp`; we reload 3 values and `jmp` to the shared latch), ~4 hot-path spill traffic (we reload a-bits twice and the exponent; gcc's three spill stores are reloaded only in the 0.14/0.15 blocks), ~1 swap copy. | M32-OS-ROT-CLK | x_prefix, x_base64, fib, x_crc32, x_radix, x_jpeg_fixed, x_idioms, sfm16_dbg (m32) | Os | clocks (bytes strict) | release/next e0c889cb7b: x_prefix 50,427 clk / 163 B | ROTATION-TRIAL: 58,870 clk / 151 B | clocks vs best of gcc13/gcc15/clang: x_prefix -0.0% -> +16.7%, x_base64 -0.9% -> +7.1%, fib +1.2% -> +9.2%, x_crc32 +1.6% -> +4.9%, x_radix +12.7% -> +17.9%, x_jpeg_fixed +2.5% -> +3.8%, sfm16_dbg +3.6% -> +6.8%, x_idioms +4.4% -> +5.0% | DEVIATE ROTATION-TRIAL keeps gcc's unrotated shape where it is smaller; the best clock in 7 of 8 cells is clang's rotated code (more bytes: x_prefix clang 182 B / 50,431 clk, gcc 134 B / 58,238, ours 151 B / 58,870). Against gcc (13 and 15 agree) ours is behind on clocks in three cells only: x_prefix +1.1%, fib +3.0%, sfm16_dbg +6.8% (best there is gcc); ahead in x_radix -1.2%, x_base64 -31%, x_idioms -38%, x_crc32 -26%, x_jpeg_fixed -46..-39%. So the first differing decision is loop rotation (the trial picks gcc's side of a bytes/clocks pair where bytes are strict at Os); the byte gap to gcc left (x_prefix +17 B: a pointer IV plus a separate count-down IV, `sub esi,1; jb` + `add edi,4`, where gcc keeps one scaled index; `-fno-lsr` -4 B only) is LSR, w12's LG3 row. sfm16_dbg (+405 clk from the trial, base already +3.6% behind gcc): cause unread. fib (+3.0% vs gcc, 10,946 clk = one extra instruction per call, read from harness --hist): the loop-exit sum is a tied `add esi,edi; mov eax,esi` where gcc writes `lea eax,[ebx+esi]`. The pin does not convert it either: TwoAddressInstructionPass converts at L1402 only if the tied source is not killed or `isProfitableToConv3Addr` holds (L784: `SrcRegMap[regB]` set by a COPY in the same block); the source `n` is a loop phi, lowered to copies in the loop blocks, so `SrcRegMap` has no entry in the exit block and `ToRegA` ($eax) alone does not suffice. `codegen/twoaddress.rs` translates the same condition. Not a translation bug; the gap is the unrotated loop's phi-fed exit sum | greedy-os-causes | 2026-12-05 | #9141 |
| M32-SFM16-OS | sfm16_dbg x86_32-wat | Os | code bytes | not stated (main 65098fd950 not re-taken here) | dist-G 294a7ec6d4: 1987 B code (+17,652 data) / 13,728 clk | gcc 13 1837 B code (+17,668) / 12,855 clk: +150 B code, +134 B total (WORSE, Os is bytes); clang 3016 B / 20,457 clk: ours is -1029 B | 2026-11-11 cause card, harness vsgcc `--hist`, per-function `nm -S` of `llrmElfOs` vs `gccOs` (same flags): bytes before the rotation trial are the same (19,640 -> 19,639 B), so the trial is not the cause; its +405 clk is M32-OS-ROT-CLK. The gap is diffuse: 14 of 27 functions are bigger (+2..+58: __divsf3 +58, add_bits +55, sf32_pack +16, bench_sfm16_dbg +12, sf_clz32 +9, __floatsisf +8, cmp_bits +7), none smaller by more than 10 (__mulsf3 -10). Instruction mix, ours - gcc: mov +30, pop +18 (`pop ecx` for `add esp,4` is a win), push +15, or +15, jae +14 (jbe -7), xor +10, setcc +15 (21 vs 6), and +7; ret count equal (19), calls 22 vs 24. Excluded: (1) the pin: its pipeline on the same C gives 3,034 B (clang 3,016), and `llc -O2` of the pin on OUR final IR gives 2,131 B text, 144 B more than ours, so the backend is not the loser; (2) the select/branch choice is priced by `sizes::cmp_sel(.., cmov)` already (no cmov at i486), the pin makes 85 selects here, ours 32 (gcc's -Os keeps branches where we keep select/setcc/or chains: add_bits block 18 `or.cond1`/`select` pairs become `setne bl; setne bh; or bh,bl; sete cl; or cl,bh; jne`). Examples read: add_bits spills both arguments at entry (`mov [esp],edx; mov [esp+4],ecx`, frame 0x1c vs gcc 0xc) where gcc keeps them in ebx/ebp; sf32_pack keeps the constant 1 (`xor edi,edi; inc edi`) in a callee-saved register across `sf_shr_sticky32` (push/pop edi and esi, +8 B) where gcc re-materialises `mov ecx,1`; the pin's llc does the same (`xor esi,esi; inc esi` live across the call), so ours matches the pin. The pin prices a first callee-saved use (`X86RegisterInfo::getCSRFirstUseCost` = 2, push+pop; translated in `greedy/targetinfo.rs`) and still keeps it; the cost is in entry-frequency units, not a byte price, which is where gcc differs. Tried 2026-11-11: (1) a byte price for the callee-saved first use at Os (DEVIATE CSR5 on): 0 of the 134 B, net +10 B over 95 programs, dropped (card csr-byte-price-null). (2) __divsf3's step `if (r>=d){r-=d;q|=1}` was folded by `twoentryphi` at exactly its 4-of-4 threshold into cmp/setae/sub-of-select; DEVIATE D6 (PR 9271, draft): -15 B here, 4 of 95 Os cells smaller, none larger, O1-O3 identical. The remaining ~119 B stay diffuse (add_bits, sf32_pack, spills at entry). | greedy-os-causes | 2026-11-27 | none |

| M16-GREP-CLK | grep (`bench/`, c frontend, m16) | O1, O2, Os | clocks (strict) | best external: bcc 33,857,865 clk (613 B) at O1 and Os | 2026-11-15 with select-as-branch (DEVIATE D5-M16; the pin's i386 keeps the two selects): O1 and O2 29,717,236 (500 B), under bcc 33,857,865; Os 37,015,409 (475 B), +9.3% vs bcc; bytes 556 (code+data) vs bcc 613. Os vs O2 (2026-11-15, stage dumps and asm): the scan loop is rotated at both; O2 header-copies the outer read loop and LSR's end-biased count-to-zero fires on the byte pointer, which `generate_end_biased` skips at -Os (`if self.size`). Measured: end-biased allowed and kept 35,968,855 clk, 476 B (-2.8%, the keep rule refuses it: 33 B against 26 B); with the rotation trial as well (rotation_guard_must_fold/trial/hot at m16, 0/4 B a loop) 35,975,190 clk, 492 B; cgpcondsplit and narrowrange at Os: no change; selectoptimize: the pin skips optsize. Closing the rest needs a bytes-for-clocks rule per loop at Os (policy, user's call). Before it: O1 34,891,879 (495 B, = O2: -ftree-pre at -O1 on the x86_16 rows, and LSR at -O1 priced in clocks where bytes are priced at speed; #9696 had regressed O1 to 46,445,077); before: head 59d624ea59 (#9577, #9620, #9628): O1 40,140,110 (498 B); Os 45,404,927 (490 B). O2 is closed: 495 B, 34,891,879 clk (+3.1% vs bcc, -9% bytes vs the 544 B bar; table row of dist 68cd2af7 predates #9620) | O1 and O2 +3.1%, Os +34.1% vs bcc; bytes 115-123 B under the bar | O1 vs O2 (2026-11-13, stage 21-pipeline IR): O1 plus `-ftree-pre` gives IR identical to O2, so the mid-end difference is GVN with PRE, which the pin's `default<O1>` lacks (`basic()` pre:false, user decision 2026-10-10): 36,989,263 clk, 506 B (-7.8%, +8 B). The #9620 spill-fold is gated off at O1/Os (bytes strict vs the parent; its O1 A/B failed the counted-cell rule); both on: O1 37,008,128 (503 B), Os 41,205,239 (494 B), leaving +9.3% O1, +21.7% Os. Loop: 7-8 values on 6 registers, bcc keeps the 32-bit counters as memory RMW; per byte ours loads, increments and stores `pos` (3 instructions) and adds a materialised `lines += inline_hit` select (`xor eax,eax` + `add edx,eax`) that bcc branches around. Not a pin divergence (m16-grep-lg3-pin-trace-2026-11-12.md). Exit work: a bytes-priced fold decision (POC on branch m16/fold-bytes2: fixed recchop and frames at O1, could not guarantee zero growth at Os) or the user's call on strict-vs-bar for O1/Os; O3 not re-measured. | Z80-SIZE-DIVIDE (DEVIATE z80-callinline-price) | copy_runs, loop_interchange, shift_int64_variable, data_int64 (z80) | Os, Oz | bytes (vs the tip; clocks fall) | tip d37cb3eade: 767 / 882 / 872 / 505 B; 729429 / 23321577 / 12343500 / 105474 T | this PR: 953 / 1068 / 1021 / 742 B; 204454 / 9655087 / 6173004 / 19038 T | +186 / +186 / +149 / +237 B; -524975 / -13666490 / -6170496 / -86436 T | a size-tuned function with a long divide takes the speed routine where the module's smaller relative regret says so (`__s_*` is 6.1k T a 32-bit divide and 99k T a 64-bit one, 4-9x the speed routine, for 76-179 B fewer). All four are ahead of the best SDCC on both axes (1518 B / 541410 T; 1237 B / 22553834 T; 1438 B / 11675066 T; 982 B / 70515 T). Typical operand lengths are assumed (24-bit dividend, 8-bit divisor; 32-bit dividend for 64-bit): a program of full-width dividends by small divisors pays more than priced. A function with a multiply or fill alternative keeps the size divide (one switch per function). Policy: -Os/-Oz take the form with the smaller relative regret (bytes against clocks, #9381), not bytes alone; this is that policy, not an unexplained growth | llrm-target-z80-c | closed 2026-11-23: each divide site is priced by its dividend's proven range (expected steps) and its divisor's high word; a function with a multiply and a divide alternative has the choice on each (`compile::settled`) | this PR |

(3) Hand `noinline` of `next`, `put32`, `emit`: 3,370 B / 49.67 M clk, under both bars; `next` alone -1,052 B / -446 k clk, because inlining it makes getbit (402 units) too big for decode (cost 285 > 225) and the hot getbit call stays. (1) Placement: the pin's `rotateLoop` leaves `jmp`s after a guarded inner loop in an outer loop (int analog too); `rotateLoopWithProfile` (`-force-precise-rotation-cost`) gives gcc's layout. Ported behind a default-off timings flag, WIP alim/m32-layout-rotate 849e4b6906, NOT landed: A/B 121 programs x 4 levels (base dbfd22b7b3) counted O1 71->73, O3 104->105, Os 29->32 but lost quicksort O1 (+3.9k clk) and x_fclip Os (+4 B), and hot loops regress (frames O1 +260k clk, x_jpeg_single O2 +274k, fib O1 +38k: the cost model rotates the test to the bottom and adds an entry `jmp`, blind to the latch tail-dup; the pin gates the pass on profile data). (2) x87 only: our LIR keeps a critical-edge block for the constant phi (`L0_2_1: fldz`) at the chain top, so the rotation cannot help the long-double cells; the pin copies `fldz` into the guard block and pops on the inner path. The block is x87 DEVIATE 7 (fpstack.rs, #2339, `_split_for_the_stack`), kept because nbody's inner-loop exit ran +5.2% instructions without it; PHIElimination is the faithful translation and is not the cause. Each is a smaller-form choice the clock-priced alternative (lea chain, stores, inline) grows: estimated, not measured: about +3 B per imul (`imul r,r,imm8` 3 B vs `lea`+`shl` 6 B), more for the fill; x_sf_i2d inlined is gcc's 774 B (+203 B). No smaller-and-faster form found, and spending slack on one cell is a row-shaped patch. Needs the user: strict bytes vs strict clocks at Os. MUL-OS off (`imul_at_size`, the pin's lea/sub/shl at Os) at 8a3bf383d1: Os counted 95 -> 95; lru -22% clk +10 B, 15 other cells +1..+4 B, none flipped: refused, the deviation stays (exit 2026-11-24).  2026-11-15 (tip 636a976f63, 121 Os cells, same build, rate 8000 vs 0): a priced partial inline of the Os trial (rate-based bound plus an Os hot trial) flips none and loses 6 counted cells on bytes (sfm16_float, sfm16_float_i2f, x_crt_i2d, x_dct, x_heapsort, x_jpeg_single; counted 101 -> 95); x_sf_i2d 84,082/587 -> 82,031/668 (rate 8000), 79,411/696 B (rate <= 1000), bar 73,910 clk and 740 B: it still makes 2 calls per iteration (`from_mag`, `sf64_pack`) where gcc inlines all at 774 B; rate <= 1000 also breaks x_sf_add (1,836 B vs 1,601). Needs the per-width table or the register-demand condition (x86-m16) | w12 | 2026-11-24 | w12/lsr-o1-insns |

Measured with the private row switched off in one binary (a kill switch, same compiler, same runtime): 1,509,947 instructions, 4,642,954 clk, 845 B against 1,509,932, 4,666,879 clk, 781 B on, so +23,925 clk (+15.95 a trip over 1500) for 15 fewer instructions. Not copies: the instruction counts are equal. The cause is operand mode: the arguments hold ten of the zero-page cells the loop body would use, so 14 more of its temporaries are spilled to static cells (`lda s_mix_N`, absolute, 4 clk and 3 B) in place of zero-page cells (3 clk, 2 B): 38 absolute accesses a trip against 24, and 1 `pha/pla` pair against 3. A trip is 455 clk on the listing (clocks by operand mode) against 439. Lever: static spill cells in zero page where the platform has room (6502-STATIC-ZP, this PR): recdeep O3 4,675,582 -> 4,624,327 clk, 692 -> 644 B. Not counted: llvm-mos 7,409,127 clk | llrm-target-6502 | 2026-11-24 | this PR |

With the pin's zero-poison price the select `x ? clz(x) : 16` folds to `ctlz(x, false)` as the pin does at i386/i486 (pin opt); the pin then despeculates it to test/je/bsr/xor and tail-duplicates the phi+ret block (pin llc i386: `test; je; bsr; xor; ret; mov $16; ret`). Ours keeps one `retf` and hoists `mov ax, 32` into the entry block, so the argument is copied off `ax` (`mov cx, ax`). | 6502-CALL-KEEPS | call_keeps (`tests/run/c`) | O1, O2, O3, Os | code bytes | cc65 1,171 / 1,134 / 1,165 / 1,134 B; llvm-mos 1,893 / 1,893 / 1,935 / 1,893 B | release/next 2dbeefed68 (2026-11-24): O1, O2, O3 1,436 B and 23,792 clk; Os 1,406 B and 24,812 clk | +265 / +302 / +271 / +272 B over cc65 (O1 / O2 / O3 / Os); clocks x1.46-1.53 over llvm-mos 16,211 | Os churn count (2026-11-23): 37 cell-to-cell copy pairs, 24 of them marshal the arguments of the 4 `__mulsi3_fast_byte` calls (a*3, b*5, c*7, d*11), so a coalescer removes at most the other 13 (~52 B); 41 `s_churn_*` spill instructions; whether expanding those four multiplies is smaller is not measured. O2 split (churn + hold + main, instruction bytes): stores to `rc` 25%, loads from `rc` 17%, ALU 17%, stores to `s_` cells 12%, loads from `s_` 5%, `jsr` 5%. cc65 per function: churn 192, hold 137, main 29 B, plus shared helpers (`pusheax`, `ldeaxysp`, `tosaddeax`, `tosxoreax`, `shreax*`). Bounds, measured before building: ALU helpers on cell numbers (`ldx #a; ldy #b; jsr`) save at most about 100 B net (73 byte-ALU ops = 18 32-bit ops, 24 B inline against about 15 B, minus helper bodies) at more clocks; long-argument marshalling (ours 16 B per long, cc65 8 B; 8 longs in `hold`) at most about 64 B; together about 165 B against +281 B. Hints are not the lever (`sta rcN; sta s_hold_K` is one def into two homes, the same bytes as a copy later; shift helpers on static scratch -10 B at Os, nothing elsewhere; imaginary-register spill (llvm-mos MOSImagRegSpill) already covered by greedy). The rest is cc65's stack-machine call convention for long arguments, an ABI price. Lever: a cc65-style convention for long arguments (design: one page with bytes and clocks of one long-argument path by 2026-11-28; cheap first step, long-argument marshalling, a selector/ABI rule; llvm-mos has none to translate, so DEVIATE) Re-read 2026-11-23 at 449313249b (Os asm of `churn`): the 13 non-marshaling pairs are spill/reload round-trips of 4-byte values through `s_churn_*` and `rc` cells, no shared shape, so the coalescer bound is <= 52 B of the +272 B gap to cc65 (1,134); the 24 marshaling pairs are policy-bound (`mul_expand_floor`, bytes first, user 2026-11-14). The other >= 220 B is not explained; no cause named. Owner w9, re-read 2026-12-18. Measured 2026-11-24 (link map and listing, cc65 -Os for the bar): ours main 49 + hold 406 + churn 724 + helpers 227 = 1,406 B; cc65 47 + 259 + 395 + 459 = 1,160 B (program +478, helpers -232). Cause: a long is four independent byte vregs (3 of 26 four-lane groups sit in contiguous cells): 944 B of ALU, cell moves and spills against 540-600 B of cc65 call sites and shared bodies. Closed as no lever: csave/crest is already byte-priced per module (`select::module_text`); direct argument-cell defs <= 16 B (rc2/rc3 hold `n` until `z` is formed); holding a frame address across calls (MachineCSE heuristics equal the pin; sharing them +180 B m64). Parked: shared 32-bit op and spill helpers, which need contiguous lane cells (lane provenance from the shared legalizer, 3+ days; best case about 1,170 B, still over the bar; design `6502-i32-cell-design-11-24.md`). | x87 | 2026-12-15 | none (design parked) |

Cause (div_constants_long Os, measured by limiting the conversions): the first 72 conversions shrink `main` 5,476 -> 5,412 B; conversion 73 flips the SD535 trial from keeping the 83 `sdiv` to expanding 27 of them (`smulh`: 56 sdiv + 27 smulh, as the pin) and `main` grows 5,412 -> 5,760 B, 5,688 with all conversions.
